Leaf is a modular benchmarking framework for federated learning, offering datasets, evaluation tools, and reference implementations to address challenges in realistic federated environments.

This paper explores using importance sampling for causal reasoning, especially counterfactual inference, within probabilistic programming. It introduces MultiVerse, a prototype engine for approximate causal reasoning, and compares it with Pyro.

This study examines how image classes are compressed through CNN layers using singular value decomposition, revealing that effective dimension first increases then decreases, correlating with model performance.

2416
A new dataset for logical entailment is introduced to evaluate models' ability to process logical structures. The study compares various architectures, finding that tree-structured networks and PossibleWorldNets outperform others in capturing logical syntax and entailment.
